    NOTE:
      Since v1.6, the G-F0m stages report first-pick share for the told-control (A0 / A0-OLMo)
      template: 8 sessions per stage, plus the single G-TIME A0 session whose picks are "reported".
      These go into a committed entry that the PI and the seat read before LOCK v1.0. That is the
      lock at which the PI sets the hour cap or declines it, and declining makes the unit NOT-RUN.
      The read is the pick-bearing preview §4's blinding rule forbids, and it opens an
      outcome-informed fork: whether a weak-looking control is published as DEAD (a fact about the
      format) or as NOT-RUN. The gate sessions are excluded from the DV, but the lock decision is
      not insulated. Fix by reporting first-pick share only for the sham template at qualification,
      or by blinding the contrast-template picks until VERDICTS. Not blocking.

      rate by the clip, and (3) and (4) only worsen with more corruption. So no rule, figure or gate
      changes, and minor is correct.

    DOCUMENT SAYS:
      :20 "Exposure in corrupted-round-bearing sessions, under §2.4's launch rule: Q-F0: Qwen 96
      launches ... 128 below it"; :153 "Q-F0: 5 looks at 192 launches"
    RECORD SAYS:
      v1.7:77: "Each arm stops launching at its own floor ... A unit's analysis runs when every arm
      has reached its floor, or its hour cap is reached, or the PI stops it". Decisions:107: "the
      campaign keeps launching ... until every arm's valid count ≥ floor or the hour cap is
      reached". box_b3_sessions.py:315 skips an arm once valid ≥ target. L is therefore neither a
      cap nor the realised count. For example, cached A0 at a campaign valid rate of .90 stops at
      about 107 launches, not 128. If the campaign rate falls below the qualification rate, launches
      continue past L up to the hour cap.
    NOTE:
      The exposure and look figures are planning values of L, not bounds on exposure or on the
      realised look count. The text does not call them bounds, and 'bounded by per-arm stopping at
      floor' is accurate, but a welfare reader would take '96/128 launches' as the exposure. A scope
      caveat, not a figure error.
